1007: function Write-LayoutError([string]$Stage,$ErrorRecord) {
1008:     try {
1009:         $msg = "$(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') [$Stage] " + [string]$ErrorRecord.Exception.Message

1266: function Randomize-Character {

1270:     # FullName is randomized first so Nickname(s) can derive from the newly generated name.

1752:     $c=Get-CurrentCharacter; if($null -eq $c){return}
1753:     $dlg=New-Object System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog; $dlg.Filter='Images|*.png;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.bmp;*.gif|All files|*.*'

1755:         try { $ext=[System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($dlg.FileName); $dest=Join-Path $script:MediaRoot ($c.Id+$ext); Copy-Item -LiteralPath $dlg.FileName -Destination $dest -Force; $c.PortraitPath=$dest; $c.Modified=(Get-Date).ToString('o'); Schedule-Save; Render-CurrentCharacter } catch { Show-Error $_.Exception.Message }
1756:     }
1757: }
